```yaml
lines:
  - id: k1s.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 ordinary business income (loss), zero if box 1 is empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 net rental real estate income (loss), zero if box 2 is empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box2}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 other net rental income (loss), zero if box 3 is empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box3}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 income (if box 1 is income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b1, take: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income (if box 2 is income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b2, take: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b3_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 income (if box 3 is income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b3, take: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss after applying the basis limitations (Form 7203, line 35, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c35}
    note: "The K-1 instructions report box 1 loss 'after applying the basis and at-risk limitations'. The basis-limited amount is read from this K-1's Form 7203, Part III, line 35, column (c). Form 7203 is required whenever a loss is claimed, so its entry is assumed to be completed. Debt basis, column (d), is not modeled."
  - id: k1s.h_b2_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss after applying the basis limitations (Form 7203, line 36, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c36}
  - id: k1s.h_b3_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 loss after applying the basis limitations (Form 7203, line 37, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c37}
  - id: k1s.h_b1_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 1 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.b1_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b2_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 2 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.b2_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b3_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss, box 3 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.b3_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_mat
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You materially participated in the corporation's activity"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.work, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.hours500, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.most_hours, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [k1s.mp_other, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.past_years, {const: true}]}
    note: "Material participation tests 1 to 7. Tests 1, 3 and the others require current-year work. Tests 5 and 6 (prior years) do not."
  - id: k1s.h_np1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 activity is nonpassive (material participation, or trading personal property for owners)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mat, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.trader, {const: true}]}
  - id: k1s.h_np2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 activity is nonpassive (real estate professional who materially participated, or personal-use dwelling rule)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.re_pro, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mat, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.dwelling, {const: true}]}
    note: "The participation questions ask about 'this corporation's business'. They are applied to the rental real estate activity for the real estate professional test."
  - id: k1s.h_np3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 activity is nonpassive (personal-use dwelling rule only)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [k1s.dwelling, {const: true}]}
    note: "Box 3 is passive for all shareholders except under the special rule for a dwelling unit a shareholder used personally. That rule is read as applying to rental activities in box 2 and box 3."
  - id: k1s.h_mfs_together
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately and lived with spouse at some time during the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [household.mfs_living, {const: "apart_all_year"}]}
  - id: k1s.h_part4_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Active participation in the rental real estate (Form 8582 Part IV)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.active, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.tenth, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mfs_together, {const: false}]}
  - id: k1s.h_p4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 activity goes in Form 8582 Part IV"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_part4_ok, {const: true}]}
  - id: k1s.h_p5b2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 activity goes in Form 8582 Part V"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_part4_ok, {const: false}]}
  - id: k1s.h_b7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 net short-term capital gain (loss), zero if empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box7}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_b8a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a net long-term capital gain (loss), zero if empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box8a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_c38
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net capital loss allowed from stock basis (Form 7203, line 38, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c38}
  - id: k1s.h_a38
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net capital loss reported (Form 7203, line 38, column (a))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.a38}
  - id: k1s.h_cap_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The capital loss is limited by basis"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_c38, k1s.h_a38]}
    note: "Form 7203 line 38 does not determine how a basis-limited capital loss splits between box 7 and box 8a. The tool refuses that case."
  - id: k1s.h_any_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This K-1 reports a loss in box 1, 2, 3, 7 or 8a"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b1, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b2, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b3, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b7, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [k1s.h_b8a, {const: 0}]}
  - id: k1s.h_atrisk
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A loss with amounts not at risk (Form 6198 needed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_any_loss, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.not_at_risk, {const: true}]}
  - id: k1s.h_k1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 nonpassive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_k2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 nonpassive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_k3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 nonpassive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b3_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_i1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 nonpassive loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_i2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 nonpassive loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_i3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 nonpassive loss"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b3_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_h1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 passive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_h2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 passive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np2, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_h3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 passive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b3_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_p5i2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 passive income reported in Form 8582 Part V"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p5b2, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p5b2, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_p5l1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 passive loss (Form 8582 Part V)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_p5l2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 passive loss (Form 8582 Part V)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p5b2, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p5b2, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_p5l3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 passive loss (Form 8582 Part V)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b3_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_p5c1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 prior-year unallowed loss (Form 8582 Part V)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b1_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np1, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_p5c2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 prior-year unallowed loss (Form 8582 Part V)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p5b2, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p5b2, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.h_p5c3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 prior-year unallowed loss (Form 8582 Part V)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_b3_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_np3, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.col_k
    printed: "28 (k)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (k): Nonpassive income from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_k1, k1s.h_k2, k1s.h_k3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.box13_other, k1s.box14, k1s.box15, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.col_i
    printed: "28 (i)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (i): Nonpassive loss allowed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_i1, k1s.h_i2, k1s.h_i3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.box13_other, k1s.box14, k1s.box15, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
    note: "Losses are taken after the basis limitation (Form 7203 column (c)). A loss with amounts not at risk is refused (Form 6198). The excess business loss limitation (Form 461) is applied on Form 1040, not on Schedule E."
  - id: k1s.col_h
    printed: "28 (h)"
    label: "Schedule E, line 28, column (h): Passive income from Schedule K-1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_h1, k1s.h_h2, k1s.h_h3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.box13_other, k1s.box14, k1s.box15, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.p4_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV, column (a): current-year net income, rental real estate with active participation (box 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_pos}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.p4_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV, column (b): current-year net loss, rental real estate with active participation (box 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_loss}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.p4_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV, column (c): prior-year unallowed loss (box 2 activity)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.h_b2_prior}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
    note: "Part IV column (c) requires active participation both in the year the loss arose and in the current year. No input asks about the earlier year. Current-year active participation is used for both."
  - id: k1s.p5_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V, column (a): current-year net income from passive activities of this corporation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_h1, k1s.h_p5i2, k1s.h_h3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V, column (b): current-year net loss from passive activities of this corporation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_p5l1, k1s.h_p5l2, k1s.h_p5l3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.p5_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V, column (c): prior-year unallowed losses of passive activities of this corporation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_p5c1, k1s.h_p5c2, k1s.h_p5c3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.interest_buy, k1s.h_atrisk]
    note: "Prior-year unallowed losses are carried only for activities that are passive this year. The text gives no treatment of a former passive activity that is now nonpassive, so it is not modeled."
  - id: k1s.int
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 4 interest income (to Form 1040, line 2b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box4}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.ord_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5a ordinary dividends (to Form 1040, line 3b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box5a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.qual_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5b qualified dividends (to Form 1040, line 3a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box5b}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.st
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 net short-term capital gain (loss) (to Schedule D, line 5)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b7}
    refuses: [k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box10, k1s.h_cap_limited, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.lt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a net long-term capital gain (loss) (to Schedule D, line 12)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.h_b8a}
    refuses: [k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box10, k1s.h_cap_limited, k1s.h_atrisk]
  - id: k1s.g28
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b collectibles (28%) gain (loss) (to 28% Rate Gain Worksheet, line 4)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box8b}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.h_cap_limited]
  - id: k1s.u1250
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8c unrecaptured section 1250 gain from the corporation's business assets (to Unrecaptured Section 1250 Gain Worksheet, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box8c}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    refuses: [k1s.box8_stmt]
  - id: k1s.backup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 13, code O, backup withholding (include on Form 1040, line 25c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box13o}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.taxexempt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16, code A, tax-exempt interest income (include on Form 1040, line 2a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.box16a}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
  - id: k1s.nonpassive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net nonpassive income (loss) from this corporation (Schedule E, line 28, column (k) minus column (i))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1s.col_k, take: k1s.col_i}
    note: "The requested interface is read as the net of the nonpassive columns for this K-1."
  - id: k1s.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 17, code V, qualified business income (loss) from the section 199A statement"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}, then: k1s.qbi_stmt}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: false}]}, then: k1s.h_zero}
    note: "The statement amount is passed through as reported. The K-1 instructions leave shareholder-specific adjustments to the Form 8995 instructions, which are not in this text."
